# Run-002 governed Archi round-trip Change Review

Review identity: **ingestion/1.0.10/run-002**, revision 1, 2026-10-20
(Australia/Sydney). Prepared by Codex. This review records detection and proposed
directives only. Every Disposition is the initial **On-Hold** default; no Solution
Architect adjudicator/date is fabricated. All Outcomes are **Unresolved**, all
reconciliation instructions are **Not authorised**, and processing is **Not yet
processed**. No architectural change, canonical-ID allocation, candidate repair,
governance edit, publication or RAD release is applied by this review.

## Review result and scope

**PASS for observed candidate semantic content, governed metadata and View content.**
The supplied export preserves all **1,394 elements**, **1,137 relationships**,
**1,209 Views**, **4,005 visual objects** and **1,804 visual connections** from the
exact proposed Run-002 candidate. Candidate → export has **0 evolved, 0 new,
0 deleted elements and 0 representation rebindings**. All **1,362 canonical
claims** retain their values, owners and bindings; the other **32 elements**
match by representation identity only and still lack canonical IDs. All three
governed names, `archimateType` and applicable `harmoniaType` values retain exact
value/language/multiplicity/owner associations. Whole-XML equivalence is **not
established**: navigation serialization and one Property-definition language
attribute differ, as classified below.

The export introduces **no additional architectural proposal relative to the
Run-002 candidate**. That candidate is not the accepted RAD baseline. This review
also exposes the inherited RAD-1.0.10 → candidate proposals at the governed human
checkpoint: **32 new Topics, 38 existing BusinessObjects retyped as Topics,
12 retained BusinessObject naming migrations, 59 new Compositions, one new
Aggregation, 11 new authored Views, and one existing View revision**. These are
**154 proposed changes**, plus **four unchanged classification findings**, indexed
as **158 review-local CRs**. They are not 158 new changes made during transport.
No earlier issued IA Change Review was found in this run; these are initial CRs,
not replacements for any issued decisions. Other releases' reviewed CRs confer
no authority here.

The general naming and projection policies are already governed. They do not
accept these architectural instances or authorise this task to migrate accepted
sources. Native Composition supplies contextual naming only conditionally on
the submitted relationships; neither names nor folders establish acceptance,
root completeness, runtime information ownership or domain authority.

## Authority and applicability

The governing [Round-Trip Engineering Praxis](../../../../../markdown/governance/archimate-round-trip-engineering-praxis.md)
and [Change Review contract](../../../../../markdown/governance/archimate-ingestion-change-review.md)
define this checkpoint. The [identity/naming contract](../../../../../markdown/governance/canonical-identity-and-naming.md),
[publication semantics](../../../../../markdown/governance/archimate-ingestion-publication-semantics.md),
[modelling strategy](../../../../../markdown/governance/architecture-modelling-strategy.md),
[navigation convention](../../../../../markdown/governance/archi-navigation-and-view-convention.md),
[relationship mapping](../../../../../markdown/governance/rad-archimate-model-mapping.md#5-relationship-profile-and-direction)
and [release policy](../../../../../markdown/governance/architecture-release-policy.md#machinery-validation-runs--2026-10-20)
retain their distinct authority. The current Home-default, native-type Composition
exceptions and standing-before-ancestry clarification takes precedence over older
broad Composition wording. All existing submitted Views remain preserved authored
presentation, including previously generated Views.

AX-04 requires reusable machinery without tool-defined meaning; AX-14 separates
semantics, identities, representation, metadata, navigation and presentation;
AX-15 preserves unproved transport/execution effects; AX-17 reserves missing
meaning and instance decisions for the Solution Architect. The CRs preserve
these constraints by recording evidence and conditional consequences, while
granting no execution or architectural acceptance. Domain04's
[concept/application distinction](../../../../../markdown/04-information-architecture/README.md#1-scope--architectural-purpose)
also makes each conceptual BusinessObject → logical Topic retyping a material
decision, despite canonical continuity. No conceptual counterpart, cross-level
mapping, database schema, FHIR resource equivalence or subsystem boundary is
inferred from a Topic name or native type.

The linked Junie workspace report was used to locate evidence only. Its recorded
PASS results and candidate description were checked against actual files and
manifests; it supplies no architectural authority. The export's deliberate
handover is the user's current Run-002 review instruction. The release remains
**1.0.10**, predecessor **1.0.9**, under the explicit machinery-validation exception.

## Exact input lineage

The requested basename `ArchiMateArchitecture.xml` does not exist. The explicitly
identified run directory contains exactly one XML export,
`ArchiMate-Architecture.xml`; that file is reviewed without moving or editing it.
No timestamp, backup, native `.archimate` file or merely similar release was used
to select an input. The root release authority, published package index and
hash-verified Run-002 manifest establish the lineage below.

The control is pinned by the existing regression fixture and Run-002 manifest;
its architectural comparison establishes all 1,362 baseline elements unchanged.
It belongs to the publication, not to the generated Run-002 candidate. It cannot
be relabelled as a no-edit control of that candidate. The user identifies the new
file as the Run-002 Archi round-trip export; exact Archi version, operator,
export profile, no-edit declaration and rendering/acceptance attestation were not
provided. Byte-level comparison cannot independently authenticate that execution
or infer whether GUI editing occurred. This limitation does not invalidate the
observed semantic/View preservation results or impose a new metadata-authoring
task on the architect.


Repository HEAD at intake: `6628a9238cd05671602e3b5350c07d7ca86aa7d2`. The existing working tree was
already modified. [provenance.json](detection/provenance.json) pins protected files
and exact live machinery SHA-256 values; review evidence records the working-tree
implementation, rather than claiming HEAD alone reproduces it.


## Detection, unchanged content and representation findings

The shared `roundtrip_praxis.observe`, `diagnose_roundtrip.Exchange/compare`,
`change_review.review`, `element_correlation.correlate`, `containment.resolve`
and `information_naming.propose` capabilities supply the observations. Secure
parsing, bundled offline XSDs, unique identifier/reference checks and exact
Property-definition-key resolution reported no input issues for the publication,
control, original IA submission, proposed candidate or supplied export. XSD
success does not establish architectural acceptance or all ArchiMate semantic
constraints. There is no new semantic resolver or comparator normalisation rule.

| Comparison | Established result |
| --- | --- |
| Candidate → supplied export | 1,394 unchanged elements; 1,362 canonical matches, 32 representation-only matches; no new/deleted/evolved elements or rebindings. |
| Publication → supplied export | All 1,362 canonical instances retained, 38 rebound/evolved, 32 new, none deleted. Of 1,362 retained instances, 1,312 keep type/Name; 12 retained BusinessObjects have governed name migrations; 38 change type/Name/specialisation. |
| Publication-controlled element observation | Helper reports 1,016 evolved / 346 unchanged because 966 records change navigation alone and 50 change naming/type content. This is a field-difference census, not 1,016 independent semantic changes. |
| Retained publication relationships after exact baseline control | All 1,077 unchanged; new population is 59 Composition plus one Aggregation. Raw changed-record counts also contain control-observed language transport. |
| Retained publication Views after exact baseline control | 1,197 unchanged; one InformationMap View changes its nodes from 1 to 11; 11 Views added. All 1,209 candidate Views survive the supplied round trip. |
| Candidate governed metadata | canonicalId: 1,362/1,362; displayName, friendlyName, canonicalName and archimateType: 1,394/1,394; harmoniaType: 249/249 exact owner/value/language/multiplicity preservation. |
| Candidate semantic relationships | All 1,137 identities, native types, endpoints, directions, Names, specialisations, Documentation, Properties and residual content preserved after resolving Property definition references by key. |
| Candidate presentation | 1,209 identities and complete View content preserved after Property-key resolution: nodes, membership, nesting, order, connections, endpoints, geometry, styles, labels, Documentation and residual content. No layout, membership or View-content difference detected. |

The raw publication-only `canonicalId` census matches by Exchange ID and counts
only 1,324/1,362 exact owners. That is **not 38 lost canonical identities**:
canonical-first correlation establishes the 38 old/new representation bindings
with the same canonical IDs. Those bindings are preserved into this round-trip
export. Exact before/after IDs, canonical IDs and XML line locators are retained
in the architectural inventory and the element CRs.

The raw candidate comparison retains **120 semantically equivalent
normalisations**, **4,917 information-loss findings**, **2,257 unexplained
differences**, **0 semantic-difference findings** and **0 comparator-classified
presentation-preserving transformations**. These are comparator findings, not
CR counts or independently established semantic losses. No residual was silently
reclassified as a generic Archi normalisation:

| Residual | Exact observation and interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| Unordered semantic collections / Property bindings | 73 keyed collection reorder findings and 47 Property-definition ID rekeys are handled by existing exact-key rules. Property values remain associated with the correct definition names. |
| Organisation identities | An independent complete inventory establishes **4,717** candidate navigation `item/@identifier` attributes and **zero** in the export. They are projection identities, separate from the retained element/relationship/View identities and canonical claims. |
| Navigation topology and membership | All **3,740 references** (1,394 elements + 1,137 relationships + 1,209 Views) retain exact labelled paths and multiplicity. All **3,598 folder paths** retain exact multiset membership. No folder/reference population or parent-child nesting loss is detected. |
| Navigation order | **215** nonempty sibling contexts change order, with unchanged immediate-child multisets. This includes navigation presentation; full ordered navigation serialization is not preserved. See the exact before/after lists in the order evidence. No order-equivalence policy is invented. |
| Positional comparator consequences | Anonymous organisation items are paired by position; reorder generates apparent subtree/label/reference losses and additions. All 4,916 organisation loss findings and all 2,257 unexplained findings remain preserved as raw evidence; the independent path inventory scopes their effects. |
| Property-definition language | `Machinery validation / Candidate standing` definition Name loses `xml:lang="en"`. Its exact text and model Property value are retained; other Property-definition content matches under existing exact-key comparison. This is a real representation-language loss, not a semantic acceptance. |

All 1,209 View navigation references and their folder paths survive, but their
organisation item IDs are lost with the rest of navigation and ordered navigation
is not wholly preserved. Thus **View content preservation** and **exact View
navigation serialization preservation** are different results. The former passes;
the latter does not. No View is regenerated or altered to hide a transport residual.

The effective current/proposed containment remains **632 Composition + 212 Home**,
with **121 historical assertions excluded**, **503 conditionally participating
unclassified assertions** across the resolution evidence and **zero containment
findings**. Currentness and conditional preview do not imply acceptance. There are
**993 containment participants**, each verified directly in the actual export to
have its own same-named folder and direct self-reference, including leaves.
[Care/Encounter extraction](detection/export-care-context.xml) proves the corrected
parent/self → child-folder/child-reference structure survives. Equipment →
CommunicationAddress remains **Aggregation**, while Workforce →
CommunicationAddress supplies the independent **Composition** context. No competing
parent is manufactured from Aggregation. Existing missing Feature ancestry and
Business Home remain missing; recorded owners and navigation establish no parent.

Shared naming derivation reproduces the submitted candidate metadata for all
**82 determinate information elements**, with **zero proposed metadata changes**.
The remaining four native DataObjects have no explicit Topic specialisation and
retain their existing metadata; they are not silently relabelled as Topics.
No Artifact population, physical-layer mapping or cross-level counterpart is
introduced. All 32 absent canonical identities remain absent without allocation.
Documentation remains preserved evidence, including historical provenance text;
it is not rewritten to agree with newly proposed type/naming changes.

## Decomposition and proposed directive boundaries

CR-001–032 cover the independently selectable new Topic instances. CR-033–070
combine each existing instance's native retyping, explicit Topic specialisation,
authored Name, Documentation evolution and representation rebinding into one
instance decision; splitting those XML differences would misstate the change.
CR-071–082 cover governed naming migration of retained BusinessObjects without
retyping. CR-083–142 separate each independently selectable semantic relationship
from endpoint acceptance and from its presentation. CR-143–154 separately review
the authored View additions/revision. CR-155–158 retain the four individual
classification questions; they are unchanged inputs, not newly detected losses.

Each relationship can be accepted or held independently of another relationship;
each View can be preserved without asserting the relationships implied by its
nesting. New elements are not bundled with their incoming/outgoing relationships.
No CR is generated for each raw XML difference, automatic naming field,
navigation item, definition rekey or visual object. Shared raw observations are
cross-referenced, and transport-only findings remain outside the architectural
CR count. No GUI edit history is inferred.

All proposed directives below are conditional drafting aids. An Accept disposition
would still require a precise Solution Architect decision, decision-linked
instruction revision, source/target applicability, actual evidenced prerequisites,
resolved scope and applicable validation. Unsupported reconciliation adapters,
unestablished source locators, ambiguous meaning, identity questions or a request
to alter another View must return for adjudication before dependent execution.
These drafts establish no universal mapping or instance acceptance. If later
authorised, metadata and navigation use the shared governed projectors; semantic
Content and existing View presentation are preserved within the accepted scope.
Publication and release remain separate gated operations.
